# LM Arena Text, raw board dumps, 20 August 2026

Captured by an agent driving the rendered page, one board at a time. The agent
died on an API error while saving Spanish, so this is what reached disk.

READ THIS BEFORE USING ANY NUMBER HERE. The boards were not all captured at the
same Style Control setting, and nothing in the rows themselves says which is
which. The marker is the plot control at the foot of each dump: a board read
with the correction ON prints "Default Leaderboard Plots", and one read with it
OFF prints "Remove Style Control Leaderboard Plots". That reads backwards, and
getting it wrong already cost a full retraction once.

What is in here, in capture order:

| board | style control | votes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Overall | off | 7,874,713 |
| Coding | off | 1,641,021 |
| Coding | off | 1,641,021 |
| Coding | on | 1,641,021 |
| Creative Writing | off | 1,199,344 |
| Creative Writing | on | 1,199,344 |
| Creative Writing | off | 1,199,344 |
| Hard Prompts | off | 3,570,555 |
| Hard Prompts | on | 3,570,555 |
| Hard Prompts | off | 3,570,555 |
| Non-English | off | 3,855,976 |
| Chinese | off | 517,940 |
| French | off | 139,318 |
| German | off | 146,929 |

The three boards captured at both settings are worth more than the rest. They
were checked against the style-controlled table the picker scores, which came
from a separate hand capture, and 14 of 14 spot values match exactly on Hard
Prompts and Creative Writing. Two independent reads of the same boards agreeing
to the point is the strongest evidence on this page that those figures are
right.

Overall, Non-English, Chinese, French and German are new and are not scored
anywhere. They are all style control off, so none of them can be mixed with the
four scored Arena figures without being recaptured with it on. Spanish was in
flight when the agent died and is absent.
